### 2026-10-03 — TASK-40.1: Dock sell e2e — sell the on-foot iron at the station terminal
The full browser DOCK SELL loop (the last substantive piece of TASK-40, and the FIRST browser run of the DockPanel + credits HUD built in 3122ad1 — it was never exercised in a browser, so this is its real verification; the server side is already covered by shard.sell + sell.ws). `tests/e2e/sell.spec.ts` mirrors cargo.spec.ts exactly (same imports, `PROTOCOL_VERSION`, raw REST+WS server prep, browser assertions, 150 s timeout, console-clean assert): claim → pad-target + terminal-target → join/warp → dev-teleport onto the pad (the pad machine docks the ship) → `POST /api/dev/give` 5 iron → close the raw client. The browser spawns IN the docked ship (`#ship-hud-cargo` + `#leave-ship-prompt` both visible) → press E to disembark (`#weight-bar` 5/40 u — 5 iron at 1 u each, in the on-foot pocket) → `POST /api/dev/teleport-char` parks the on-foot character 1.5 m from the terminal (the disembark spawn is ~15.5 m out, past BOTH the 3 m interact AND the 10 m sell range, so the walk is teleported) → turn ('d', falling back to 'a' — one 3 rad/s sweep must cross the ±30° cone) until `#interact-prompt` shows '[E] Dock terminal' → press E. The server's 'ui-open' {ui:'dock'} frame opens `#dock-panel` fully populated (hold + inventory ride the payload — no cargo_open round trip): the iron row shows the base price 5 cr/u and 'hold 0 · inv 5'. `#credits-counter` is boot-seeded to 500 cr (GET /api/players/me). Click 'sell all iron inv' → the client's 'sell' frame → the server's 'sell' RESULT frame re-renders the panel (the inventory row empties to "Nothing to sell") AND updates the counter to 500 + 5×5 = 525 cr within one frame, plus the '+25 cr' CreditFloatLayer float.
- **E2E:** `tests/e2e/sell.spec.ts` (green ~10 s, desktop chromium) — the FULL loop above in a single test. Screenshot `.ralph/screenshots/TASK-40-1.png` shows the STATION DOCK panel (SELL tab, 'credits: 525', 'Nothing to sell') + the top-right counter '525 cr' + the '+25 cr' float + the '[E] Dock terminal' prompt + the emptied 0/40 u weight bar.
- **Client bugs:** NONE — the DockPanel/credits HUD wiring from 3122ad1 (ui-open → openDockPanel, 'sell' result → applySellResult + setCredits + pushCreditFloat, boot seed via /api/players/me) ran correct on the first browser run; no fix was needed in `src/client/`.
- **TSC fix (pre-existing, unblocking 'tsc --noEmit clean'):** `src/server/shard/shard.sell.test.ts` had a circular `typeof repo` self-initializer in the `makeRepo` stub (the `withTransaction` param was `typeof repo`, which tsc cannot resolve inside the const's own initializer → `repo` inferred `any`, TS7022). It was present on the clean pre-task commit (verified by stashing the new spec and re-running tsc) — the 33 server tests still ran green under vitest (which strips types) so only `tsc` caught it. Fixed by extracting a named `SellRepoStub` interface (the repo surface the sell path touches) and annotating `const repo: SellRepoStub` + using it in `withTransaction` — a type-annotation-only change (zero runtime impact); the 33 sell/terminals/shard.sell/sell.ws tests re-ran green afterwards.
- **Verify:** `npx tsc --noEmit` clean; eslint + prettier clean on both touched files; `npm run test:e2e sell.spec.ts` green (final run 9.9 s); the 33 server sell tests re-ran green (4 files / 33 passed). The full `npm run test` suite is TASK-40.2's job (deliberately not re-run here).

### 2026-10-02 — TASK-39: Cargo hold model with transfer rules
The ship's cargo hold: `{stacks, weightUsed, capacity}` where capacity = class `cargoSlots × 10` (scout 40 / interceptor 20 / freighter 120 — one shared stat drives both UI and math). `src/shared/cargo.ts` owns the model + atomic `transferCargo` (load/unload, PARTIAL when space runs out, per-resource amounts validated server-side) + `parseCargoJson`/`sanitizeCargo`. Persistence: `000004_ship_cargo.sql` adds `ships.cargo` JSON (COALESCE upsert — a cargo-less flush never wipes it). Shard: `handleCargoOpen` (own-ship only; in-ship shows hold-only, on-foot within 5 m shows hold + inventory) and `handleCargoTransfer` (validates regime → docked → range → ownership → resource → amount, one atomic move: hold/inventory entity + character mirror + shared `cargo-transfer` event). Warp carries the hold — the router now writes the fresh hold onto the adopted entity BEFORE the next flush can clobber it (bug found by the warp round-trip test: `adoptEntity` was reading the stale flush-behind row; fix in router.ts ~line 388). Client: cargo panel (two columns, move 1 / move all per resource, weight bars) reachable on foot via the prompt sub-zones (≤ 3 m '[E] Enter ship' / 3–5 m '[E] Open cargo') and from the ship HUD CARGO button (hold-only while in flight — the TASK-51 stub surface). Selling-from-hold is TASK-40.
- **Unit:** `shared/cargo.test.ts` (19: capacity 40/20/120 per class, transfer validation ladder, atomic PARTIAL at cap, sanitize) + `shard.cargo.test.ts` (9: open regimes, not-docked/out-of-range/not-owner, transfer ladder, load PARTIAL 39→40 cap, unload bounded by stow AND the 40 u inventory cap, interact 'open-cargo' on another player's ship → not-owner) + `persist.test.ts` (+2: cargo rides ships.cargo, restart round trip flush → row → `loadShips` → 13 u / 40 cap).
- **WS:** `cargo.ws.test.ts` (2 live-WS tests: transfer round trip + insufficient + not-docked + two-player isolation (no 'cargo' frame leak, not-owner) + wrong-regime; warp round trip — load 10 iron, warp A→B, hold still 10).
- **E2E:** `tests/e2e/cargo.spec.ts` (23.5 s) — raw-WS claim → dock → give 10 iron → in-ship CARGO button → hold-only panel → disembark → walk into the 3–5 m band → Open cargo → move-all-iron → INVENTORY 'empty', CARGO HOLD 10/40 u. Screenshots `.ralph/screenshots/TASK-39-1.png` (in-ship hold-only) / `TASK-39-2.png` (on foot, loaded).
- **Verify:** full suite 1025 passed / 1 skipped; the only 3 reds were load-sensitive performance benchmarks (transitionCycle p99 4.92 ms vs 4, streaming-benchmark slice p99 9.52 ms vs 8, surface.test 5 s timeout) under 119-file parallel load — all three pass 2× in isolation (19/19), no cargo or protocol involvement; the two previously-red files (`repo.test.ts` migration count, `schemas.test.ts` coverage lint) are green. tsc + eslint + prettier clean. Addendum (iteration 7, 21:53 UTC): a FRESH full-suite run was fully green — 119 files / 1028 passed / 1 skipped, 0 reds — and `tsc --noEmit` re-verified clean. The load-sensitive benchmark class was further characterized: `multiplayer-foot.ws.test.ts` (scale p95) and `persist.test.ts` (perf guard) pass in isolation and the scale test ALSO fails on the pre-TASK-39 parent commit `c4f66b7` (6.55 ms > 4 ms) — pre-existing borderline timing tests under this machine's documented CPU jitter (TASK-30's 48 % floor), not TASK-39 regressions; TASK-39's diff adds zero per-tick work (all new shard code is message-driven).

### 2026-10-02 — TASK-38: Mining: on-foot deposit extraction with a channel
On foot, holding E on a deposit in range mines it: a 1.5 s server-side channel per unit with a radial progress HUD, weight-capped intake, and shared-deposit depletion. The channel state `{playerId, depositId, unitsSoFar, lastAwardAt}` lives in the shard (`shard.mining`); interact actions extend the protocol (`mine-start` on E-down, `mine-stop` on E-up, `mine-tick` = idempotent re-assert, legacy `pickup`/absent accepted). The TICK is the only award path (`updateMining` → shared pure `stepMiningChannel`, `MINING_UNIT_MS = 1500`): a unit lands exactly when `now − lastAwardAt ≥ 1.5 s` — a client can spam 20 `mine-tick` messages in a second and gain nothing extra (anti-spam AC). Each award is atomic (deposit −1 / inventory +1 via TASK-34's `pickupInto`; seed rows persist via TASK-37's `persistDeposit`; despawn + `'depleted'` end at 0), the range re-checks per tick (`> 3 m` → `'cancelled'`), and at the 40 u cap the channel PAUSES (`status: 'full'` echo, `lastAwardAt` deliberately held — the award lands the tick space frees). Progress is echoed server-timed at 10 Hz on a PRIVATE `mining` frame (`phase: 'active' {progress, units, status}` | `phase: 'ended' {reason: stopped|cancelled|depleted, units}`) so client clocks can never desync the ring; the deposit quantity itself rides the SHARED 10 Hz snapshot to every peer. Client: `input/interaction.ts` sends `mine-start`/`mine-stop` from the E keydown/keyup (+ window-blur release, `e.repeat` ignored), `state/mining.ts` + `ui/mining-hud.tsx` render the conic-gradient radial ring + ore counter + '+1 iron' float + 'Backpack full' / 'Depleted' prompts around the 'Hold [E] to mine iron' prompt. Steps 1–3 shipped in the prior iteration's wip commit; this iteration closed it: the 1-line absent-action back-compat fix in `handleMine`, the `shard.deposits.test.ts` character-fall fix (the character is now created by the REAL dock → `handleExitShip` flow instead of `addEntity`, which left it in the ship loop's `playerEntities` integration; also moved the depletion test to the 13-unit seq 2 deposit — the 45-unit one can never fully drain under the 40 u cap), and the full step-4 test pass below.
- **Unit:** `shared/mining.test.ts` (5: cadence, partial at cap, held-award semantics) + `shard.mining.test.ts` (11, fake clock: cadence, anti-spam 20 ticks = no gain, cancel/range-loss/re-entry/disconnect kill, weight-cap pause + held award, depletion despawn, two-miner atomicity, resource types, deposit switching) + `shard.interact.test.ts` (channel-based deposit branch, exact-3 m boundary starts the channel) + `shard.deposits.test.ts` (mine 5 → restart → remaining = initial − 5, depletion despawn with row kept at 0) + `interaction.test.ts` (mine-start frame, E-release → mine-stop, new prompts).
- **WS:** `interact.ws.test.ts` rewritten for hold-to-mine (A holds → PRIVATE `mining` frame to A only — B's buffer contains zero `mining` frames — quantity 2→1 on the SHARED snapshot for BOTH, `mine-stop` → `reason: 'stopped'`, second hold → despawn for both + `reason: 'depleted'`) + NEW `mine.ws.test.ts` (live ws, real time: full channel + cancel awards nothing further, spam 20 `mine-tick` bursts gain nothing — exactly 1 unit per full channel, weight-cap pause `status: 'full'` → drop 1 iron → held award lands, two miners on ONE unit — despawn + exactly one award across both `'depleted'` ends).
- **E2E:** `tests/e2e/mining.spec.ts` — raw-WS claim → pad → dock → disembark → browser on foot → `POST /api/dev/deposit` quantity 5 (iron) at `__CHAR__.pos + (0,0,1.5)` (30° cone) → 'Hold [E] to mine iron' prompt → hold E → radial `#mining-hud` mid-channel → 2 server awards bring `#weight-bar` to 2/40u → release E → HUD hidden, deposit at 3. Screenshots `.ralph/screenshots/TASK-38-1.png` (mid-channel ring + counter 0) / `TASK-38-2.png` (2/40u after release).
- **Verify:** full suite 116 files / 987 passed / 1 skipped, e2e green (10.9 s), tsc + eslint + prettier clean.

### 2026-10-02 — TASK-37: Resource deposits — seeded placement + persistence + ore-rock rendering
Resource deposits (iron / copper / rare-earth / crystal) are placed deterministically per system from the seed and their remaining quantities persist so mining depletes them for everyone. `depositsFor(seed, system)` (shared/world/deposits.ts) scatters up to 120 deposits (10–50 units each) onto solid habitable/rocky terrain — cell-aligned, ≥ 200 m spacing, slope ≤ 30°, `pos.y` = heightAt exactly, resources weighted 40/30/20/10 — identical on client and server (positions are DERIVED, never stored; the DB keeps only deltas: `000003_deposits.sql` with PK `(system_id, deposit_seq)`, row created lazily on the FIRST mine, depleted deposits despawn with their row kept at 0). The shard spawns the 120 deposit entities, streams only seeded deposits inside the 500 m ring (dev-hook deposits always ride — snapshots stay lean), flips `depositDiscovered` at ≤ 50 m (per-tick sweep + `deposit-discovered` event), and persists quantity deltas on the mine path (restart test: mine 5, restart, remaining = initial − 5). Client (step 3, closed this iteration): the ore-rock layer (client/world/ore-rocks.ts) renders low-poly dodecahedron rocks colored per resource from the shared catalog (shared/resources.ts — the one definition of weights/prices/colors), streams the 500 m ring per frame, and pulses the emissive while < 10 units remain. Two client bugs found and fixed by the e2e: the wire id is `deposit:<depositId>` (prefix now the shared `DEPOSIT_ENTITY_PREFIX` constant) so mined quantity deltas now actually match the derived list, and dev-hook deposits (absent from the derived list) render via a wire-supplied "external deposit" path (pos/resourceId/quantity from the snapshot). The `__DEPOSITS__` dev hook (client/deposits-debug.ts, DEV-only, same pattern as `__CHAR__`) exposes the rendered rocks.
- **Unit:** `deposits.test.ts` (placement determinism same-seed→identical across 3 systems, spacing ≥ 200 m, surface placement heightAt ± 0.5, catalog weights/prices, 10..50 amounts) + `shard.deposits.test.ts` (spawn 120 entities, discovery sweep ≤ 50 m, snapshot 500 m ring filter, mine → persist → restart overlay, despawn-at-zero with row kept).
- **E2E:** `deposits.spec.ts` — raw-WS dock + disembark (walk.spec.ts pattern), on foot: asserts ≥ 1 SEEDED ore rock visible inside the 500 m ring AND rocks beyond the ring hidden (streaming, not rendering), then `POST /api/dev/deposit` at the character's `__CHAR__` position (quantity 3 → pulses) streams in within one 10 Hz snapshot and renders 1.5 m in front. Screenshots `.ralph/screenshots/TASK-37-1.png` (walking near ore rocks) / `TASK-37-2.png` (placed deposit visible); `transitions.spec.ts` re-run green — the cold `depositsFor` in `swapWorld` does not trip the frame budget.
- **Handoff fixes:** root-caused + fixed the `multiplayer-foot.ws.test.ts` regression (PRE-EXISTING racy test — a stale `docked` wire regime from the home-dock rest, reproduced on the parent commit ~1/4 runs; the docked waits now require `regime === 'docked' && padId !== undefined`, the real pad dock `handleExitShip` checks), plus the two missed 120-entity assertions in `shard.ws.test.ts` (size-before-close compare) and `shard.character.test.ts` (filter `kind !== 'deposit'` instead of the wrong `playerId` filter).
- **Verify:** full suite 113 files / 964 passed / 1 skipped, e2e green (deposits 9.3 s + transitions 8.5 s), tsc + eslint + prettier clean.

Every ship regime transition is verified to hitch under 4 ms: the scripted cycle — space to atmosphere, atmosphere to surface, disembark, walk 10 m, re-enter, surface to atmosphere, atmosphere to space — runs the real client pipeline headless and every one of the 7 phases stays under 4 ms delta over its per-phase baseline, with 0 budget warnings, no frame > 100 ms, and the TASK-26 7x7 pre-gen pad near ring verified ready 868 frames before arrival. Implementation shipped across `30ee531` + `ac84562` (the cycle harness `src/client/test/transitionCycle.ts`, the CI test, the bench script `npm run bench:transitions`, the e2e spec, and the `installTransitionDebug()` dev hook in `main.tsx`); TASK-30.1 re-verified the whole matrix on a fresh session (tsc clean, CI test 5/5 in 2.29 s, bench PASS, e2e 19.3 s, full suite 107 files / 919 passed / 1 skipped, eslint + prettier zero errors) and this iteration closes the task: recorded the fresh numbers below, flipped the step flags (all 4 in the TASK-30 spec, restored to `.ralph/tasks/TASK-30.json` — the split commit had moved it to `.ralph/split/TASK-30/`) and `passes: true` in `tasks.json`, and removed the handoff.

The bench therefore implements BOTH the strict gate and the recorded floor clause (spread < 2× session floor: 48.8% < 96.2%), and the floor clause was the one that passed. **TASK-61's reference hardware is the final variance authority** — run the same bench there and apply the strict 20% gate.

### 2026-10-02 — TASK-35: Re-entry — return to ship seamlessly
The reverse of disembark: within 5 m of the player's own idle/docked ship the '[E] Enter ship' prompt appears, and E switches the player back to the ship with the 600 ms camera handoff to the cockpit. Resumed from a cut-short iteration (implementation already in the tree, e2e green); this pass fixed the one stale test expectation, re-verified everything, and shipped.
- **Server `enter_ship` (step 1)** — `shard.ts`: new `handleEnterShip(playerId, shipId, source)` with validation order not-found → not-owner (v1: owner only, no boarding) → already-in-ship (idempotent: no `char:<playerId>` entity → no duplicate state) → out-of-range (> 5 m from the CHARACTER position) → ship-moving (velocity >= 1 u/s; an idle drifting ship can still be re-claimed). On success: deletes the character entity, clears `disembarked`, drops the held input frame (no ghost thrust), un-idles, emits the `entered-ship` event. `handleInteract`'s 'ship' branch now DELEGATES to it (the TASK-33 stub is gone); `routeGameMessage` routes the new `enter_ship` message; `InteractOutcome`/`EnterShipOutcome` unions carry the new codes. Shared tunables in `src/shared/interaction.ts`: `ENTER_SHIP_RANGE_M = 5`, `ENTER_SHIP_MAX_SPEED = 1`, `interactRangeFor(kind)` — `nearestInteractable` uses per-kind reach when `range` is omitted (ship 5 m, everything else 3 m), client `resolveInteract` passes the same default.
- **Client prompt + handoff (step 2)** — the registry's ship entry now sends `enter_ship` (prompt '[E] Enter ship', eligibility = own callsign only); `WorldManager.reEnterShip(pos, quat)`: the first call of the transition disposes the character capsule and runs the reverse 600 ms camera handoff (`cameraRig.handoff('cockpit')`, the TASK-27 rig in reverse), later 10 Hz self updates just feed the pose. `main.tsx` self-entity bridge: `self.kind === 'ship'` → `world.reEnterShip(...)`; the not-on-foot branch clears the character predictor + interaction prompt.
- **Regression fixed this pass** — the previous session's uncommitted `main.tsx` refactor cleared `charPredictorRef.current` on EVERY self-entity update (including on-foot), killing the prediction loop that is the only sender of on-foot input frames (walking, turning AND the interaction raycast all died; `walk.spec.ts` e2e was failing). The clear + prompt teardown now live in the not-on-foot branch only; `walk.spec.ts` green again.
- **Tests (step 3)** — NEW `shard.enter-ship.test.ts` (7, through the real SimLoop: happy path, idempotency, not-owner, 5 m boundary inclusive, 1 u/s boundary, idle-off-pad re-claim, not-found) and NEW `galaxy/enter-ship.ws.test.ts` (3 live-ws: disembark→re-enter round trip with no orphan character + position consistency + double-enter denial, not-owner player B, ship-moving rejection). Also fixed the one stale expectation in `shard.interact.test.ts`: the second interact after a successful enter is denied by the regime check first (no `char:p1` → `'wrong-regime'`, not `'already-in-ship'` — that idempotent denial is owned by `handleEnterShip` directly, covered by the two new files). E2E — NEW `tests/e2e/enter-ship.spec.ts`: dock over raw WS → browser disembarks (E) → spins 'd' until '[E] Enter ship' appears (the character spawns 2.5 m to the ship's SIDE — facing must be found, not assumed) → walks W 10 m (prompt hides) → walks back to the start position (within 0.6 m — a fixed backward distance overshoots the 5 m radius) → E → docked HUD stubs back, 600 ms reverse handoff to the cockpit.

Built across commits 5c20895 + a0a4ff7 (iteration 19), ea8afaf + f89fbdb (close-out); this pass re-verified the full suite and shipped.

Repo: `getPlayerInventory` / `updatePlayerInventory` + `players.inventory` JSON column (migration `000002_player_inventory.sql`); loaded on join, flushed on the TASK-24 cadence.

- **Tests (step 4)** — unit: `shared/inventory.test.ts`, `shard.inventory.test.ts` (9: partial pickup at the 40-cap boundary, drop+re-pickup round trip, ttl expiry, persistence across a simulated restart); WS: `galaxy/inventory.ws.test.ts` (2: two players, one drops, the other sees the ground item over the wire). ### 2026-10-02 — TASK-33: On-foot interaction — prompt, pick up, examine
The full on-foot interaction loop: the per-frame raycast prompt, the server-validated `interact` with per-kind effects, and the InteractableRegistry as the single dispatch site. Resumed from a cut-short iteration (working tree already had shared math + client registry + server handler in place; this pass finished the client wiring, wrote all the tests, and shipped).
- **Shared contract** (`src/shared/interaction.ts`) — `INTERACT_RANGE_M = 3` / `INTERACT_CONE_DEG = 30` (half-angle), `interactForward` (local +Z under a yaw-only quat, degenerate-safe), `inInteractRange` / `inInteractCone` (both inclusive, 3-D), `nearestInteractable` (nearest in range AND cone; bit-equal distances break on the SMALLER id → list-order independent). BOTH sides anchor at the character position, so the client pre-filter can never present a target the server would reject on range.
- **Client** (`src/client/input/interaction.ts` NEW, `ui/interact-prompt.tsx` NEW, `main.tsx`) — `InteractableRegistry`: Map<kind, {prompt, eligible, onInteract}>; the v1 entries are deposit ('[E] Take ore' → `interact {targetId, action:'pickup'}`), own ship ('[E] Enter ship', eligible ONLY for the player's own callsign — no boarding others), terminal ('[E] Dock terminal'). `resolveInteract` = registry pre-filter (the prompt only ever shows VALID targets) + the shared nearest raycast; `nextPromptState` = emit-on-change show/hide/switch (same object back when unchanged → the rAF loop re-renders React only on a real change). `main.tsx`: the target list tracks every `entity_update` batch + system snapshots (`interactableTargetsFrom` — the sparse seed entities, never the scene graph); the TASK-32 rAF loop runs the raycast at the predictor's feet + facing; the E key dispatches through `registry.dispatch` (the ONLY 'interact' send — enforced by a lint-style test that greps src/client); the prompt clears on snapshot, on non-on-foot, and on `ui-open` frames (logged until TASK-40/53 consumes them).
- **Server** (`shard.ts`, `shards.ts`, `types.ts`, `routes/dev.ts`, `schemas.ts`) — `routeGameMessage` routes `interact` → `shard.handleInteract`: validation order wrong-regime (no `char:<playerId>`) → not-found (unknown id or non-interactable kind) → out-of-range (> 3 m from the CHARACTER, stale-conn guarded errors like exit_ship); dispatch by kind: deposit = the v1 pickup (quantity −1, despawn at zero, `pickup` event — the delegate point TASK-38 replaces with the 1.5 s channel), terminal = a `ui-open` {ui:'dock', payload:{terminalId}} frame to the requester ONLY (wire-validated before send, generic shape for future terminals), ship = TASK-35 stub (validation live, no effect). SimEntity gains kinds `deposit` / `terminal` + optional `quantity` (wire-visible: a pickup shows as a quantity change — or a removal at zero — in every client's next 10 Hz snapshot); static interactables are in `entities` but never `playerEntities`, so the tick leaves them exactly put. `POST /api/dev/deposit` (dev-only e2e hook — seeded placement lands in TASK-37).
- **Protocol** (`schemas.ts`) — `interact.action` is now optional (the kind dispatch is server-side); `entityStateSchema` accepts kinds `deposit` / `terminal` + optional non-negative integer `quantity`; new server-only `ui-open` message.
- **Tests**: unit — `shared/interaction.test.ts` (16: forward math, 3 m inclusive boundary, 30° inclusive cone, side/behind exclusion, 3-D distance + cone, nearest/order-independence + id tie-break, custom range/cone), `client/input/interaction.test.ts` (14: registry dispatch + unknown-kind no-op, AC prompt strings, own-ship pre-filter, yaw rotates the cone, snapshot → target list filtering, the full show/hide/switch state machine incl. emit-on-change identity, the lint-style single-dispatch-site grep), `client/ui/interact-prompt.test.tsx` (renders only with a target). Server — `shard.interact.test.ts` (6, real SimLoop: all three error codes incl. requester-only delivery, exactly-3 m acceptance, pickup decrement/despawn/events/wire quantity, ui-open to the requester only, the ship stub). WS — `galaxy/interact.ws.test.ts` (live ws: TWO clients, one picks up, BOTH see the quantity −1 in the same snapshot stream and BOTH see the removal at zero; out-of-range denial over the wire). E2E — `tests/e2e/interact.spec.ts` (server-side dock + disembark + dev deposit 1.5 m in front of the character → the browser shows '#interact-prompt' with '[E] Take ore' → press E → prompt hides within one snapshot, console clean).
- Verified: `npm run test` green twice (101 files, 878 passed / 1 skipped; one unrelated flake on a first load-heavy run, green on the next two full runs); `interact.spec.ts` e2e green (7 s); `tsc --noEmit` clean; eslint + prettier clean on all touched files.

### 2026-10-01 — TASK-32: On-foot character movement: walk, run, jump, terrain
Server-authoritative on-foot movement through the real sim + the first browser input path in the game (flight input was never sent by the real client). Resumed from a cut-short iteration 15 (handoff: steps 1-2 done/committed, 3-4 pending).
- **Shared physics** (`src/shared/physics/character.ts`, step 1, committed d1fd9b5) — `integrateCharacter(state, input, dt, heightAt?)`: walk 3 / run 6 u/s (arcade speed-set), ground friction 8 u/s², jump 5 u/s only from ground (apex exactly 25/24 m at g 12 — the spec's "1.2 s airtime" contradicts its own pinned 1.04 m apex, the AC won, documented in the header), NO air control (move AND turn ignored airborne), terrain lerp 20 u/s, substeps ≤ 1 m horizontal travel, walk-off-edge → projectile fall, silent landing. `src/shared/protocol/inputs.ts` adds `inputToCharacterInput` (thrust→fwd/back ±0.5, yaw→left/right, action 'run'/'jump'/'run+jump' — the SAME 'input' message drives both regimes, no protocol change).
- **Sim routing** (step 2, committed 577f6f7) — `shard.ts` tick: second per-entity loop for `kind === 'character'` consumes the owner's held input frame (same pattern as ships), integrates with the pad+terrain `heightAt` (char keeps `ship.regime = 'surface'`), writes back to `entity.ship` + `charOnGround`; `enqueueInput` accepts disembarked players' frames; `handleExitShip` inherits planetId/livery; disconnect clears the char's held frame (coast to stop).
- **Physics fix found by the new fixture** — seeded terrain is integer-metre heights on a 5 m grid, so LOCAL slopes can beat the 20 u/s lerp budget on steep uphills (the "max 45°" assumption doesn't hold per-cell). Added the hard floor the header already promised: on the ground the feet are clamped onto `heightAt` (no sink-in, substep-limited); all pinned constants unchanged.
- **Client predictor** (`src/client/net/character-prediction.ts`, NEW, step 3) — `CharacterPredictor` mirroring `ClientShipPredictor` (same seq/ack/reconcile machinery: server-timeline replay when age known, local-currency fallback — the v1 default, blend/rewind/snap, 10 s queue cap) over the SHARED `integrateCharacter`; `characterStateFromWire` for the 10 Hz self snapshot.
- **Client wiring** (`main.tsx`) — the first input-send loop in the browser: pressed-key set (WASD/Shift/Space; typing in inputs ignored, blur clears) → 'input' frames on change or at 20 Hz (re-echo keeps the server's held frame + acks current); predictor seeded from the first self character entity_update (terrain = the flat pad plane via new `WorldManager.characterPadHeight`) and reconciled on every self update against the last APPLIED seq (new `onAck` hook — the client previously ignored 'ack' frames). Per-frame rAF drives `WorldManager.setCharacterTransform(pos, quat?, livery?)`: the character model is now a capsule body + head (group origin at the FEET, facing quat, livery hull/accent re-tint — cosmetic tie-in per spec). Remote characters already ride the generic 200 ms interpolation buffer (no change).
- **WorldManager** (`WorldManager.ts`) — `buildCharacterMesh` → capsule + head group (feet origin), `setCharacterTransform`/`applyCharacterTransform`/`setCharacterLivery`, `characterPadHeight` getter; dispose paths updated (shared `disposeGroup`).
- **Tests**: unit — `character.test.ts` (17: speeds, friction stop, turn math, jump apex 25/24, no double jump, no air control, 30° slope <0.1 m, ledge walk-off, 45° kink no-tunnel, determinism), `inputs.test.ts` (wire mapping), `character-prediction.test.ts` (10: shared-model lock step, held input + friction stop, 30° slope tracking, jump arc, reconcile blend/rewind/snap/cap + 150 ms RTT latency sim — every post-warmup correction blends, end gap < 5 u). Integration — `shard.character.walk.test.ts` (NEW): the 30 s scripted AC — walk 8 s / run 5 s / turn 2 s / jump-walk 3 s / idle 4 s / back+turn 8 s through the REAL SimLoop (~87 m path, leaves the 20 m pad disc onto real chunk terrain), sampled every 1 s → committed fixture `__fixtures__/character-walk-30s.json` (regen: `CHAR_WALK_FIXTURE=1 npx vitest run …walk.test.ts`); asserts the path is bit-identical, never below the surface in any of the 600 ticks, and ends grounded on the terrain.
- **E2E** (`tests/e2e/walk.spec.ts`, NEW) — dock server-side (disembark flow), browser disembarks via E, holds W 2.5 s: the SERVER-authoritative position (dev-only `window.__CHAR__` hook, `src/client/char-debug.ts` — one connection per player so no second raw client) advances ≥ 4 m (walk = 3 u/s ≈ 7 m), y stays on the pad plane, console clean.

### 2026-10-01 — TASK-31: Disembark — exit ship to on-foot character (server + client + e2e)
The first surface-mode entry point: from a pad-docked ship the player presses E to disembark — a static character entity spawns 2.5 m to the ship's side on the pad plane, the ship stays docked (frozen) and its inputs are dropped, and the client hands the shared camera off to the on-foot third-person view (TASK-27). Resumed from a cut-short iteration 5 (handoff at `.ralph/handoff/TASK-31.md`); the working tree already contained the full implementation — this pass verified, linted, and shipped it.
- **Shared spawn math** (`src/shared/physics/character.ts`, NEW) — `characterSpawnPos(shipPos, shipQuat, padHeight)`: ship world pos shifted 2.5 m along its world-right (forward-perpendicular, `CHAR_SHIP_SIDE_OFFSET_M`), y pinned to the pad plane. One definition used by the sim (authority), the tests, and the e2e — so all three agree by construction.
- **Server** (`shard.ts`): `handleExitShip(playerId, shipId, source)` — must own the ship (else `{code:'unknown-ship'}`), must be pad-docked (else `{code:'not-docked'}` to the requesting conn only, stale-conn guarded), else a static `character` entity (`id char:<playerId>`, surface regime, zero vel, identity quat) is added to `entities` (NOT `playerEntities` — it owns no ship-input lane) at the shared-math position, the ship is flagged `disembarked` (tick skips it → frozen, no drift / re-dock churn; `enqueueInput` drops its frames), and a `disembarked` event is emitted. A disconnect while on foot leaves the character in the sim (TASK-24 pattern) and re-adopt restores the on-foot state. `entityToState` now emits `playerId` + `onFoot` for `kind 'character'` (schema: `schemas.ts` adds those optional fields).
- **Dispatch** (`shards.ts` + `index.ts`): new `routeGameMessage(shard, conn, type, payload)` is the single gameplay dispatch surface (input queue / chat / `exit_ship`) — production entry and the live-ws tests both call it, so the routing has one definition.
- **Client** (`main.tsx` + `WorldManager.ts` + `leave-ship-prompt.tsx`): the self entity is resolved CHARACTER-first (after disembark the frozen ship still carries the callsign; the character's `onFoot` flag is what the client acts on). `WorldManager.setCharacterPos` spawns a placeholder capsule (2.1 m, TASK-32's model replaces it) + feeds the CameraRig + runs the 600 ms `handoff('onfoot')`; `clearCharacter` undoes it. `LeaveShipPrompt` renders "E — LEAVE SHIP" bottom-center exactly while the docked store is true (same store as `#docked-indicator`); the E handler in `useGameSession` sends `exit_ship` only when the prompt is up, the chart is closed, and not typing in an input.
- **Tests**: unit — `character.test.ts` (spawn math: identity / yawed / altitude cases), `shard.character.test.ts` (real SimLoop: dock then exit → char at the shared-math pos on the pad plane, ship stays docked, frozen ship drops inputs even from a smuggled held frame, `not-docked` / `unknown-ship` denials, double-exit no-op, disconnect-while-on-foot persistence + re-adopt), `leave-ship-prompt.test.tsx` (visible iff docked). WS integration — `src/server/galaxy/exit-ship.ws.test.ts` (live ws: character appears at ship pos + offset ± 0.5 m, on the pad plane, ship stays docked in the SAME snapshot, two clients see the exact same position, denied when not docked with no character ever). E2E — `tests/e2e/disembark.spec.ts` (full flight → dock → press E → on-foot: HUD stubs disappear, capsule dead-center, on-foot screenshot; `helpers.ts` gains `canvasMaxLuminance` to prove the flat-colored capsule where variance alone can't).
- Verified: `npm run test` green (93 files, 806 passed / 1 skipped); `npm run test:e2e` green (14/14, ~2.8 min); `tsc --noEmit` clean; eslint + prettier clean on touched files.
- Screenshot: `.ralph/screenshots/TASK-31-1.png` (on-foot: cyan character capsule dead-center on the pad, docked HUD stubs gone)

## Session Log

### 2026-10-01 — TASK-29.2: VTOL-assisted approach — scripted 100 m → docked in < 15 s (sim convergence test)
The last TASK-29 sim acceptance criterion is met: a scripted approach that STARTS 100 m horizontally from the seeded pad converges to 'docked' in **90 ticks × 50 ms = 4.5 s of sim time** (budget 15 s), through the REAL SimLoop with the committed server-side VTOL assist — no production changes (the assist constants 0.5 / 100 m / 50 u/s are untouched spec).
- `app/src/server/shard/shard.pads.approach.test.ts` (NEW, 2 tests; shard.pads.test.ts conventions: stub repo/bus/log, addEntity + registerConnection, one tick per sim.step, pad position from `padsForSystem` only — never hardcoded): (1) the full approach — asserts 100 m start below the 1 km atmosphere boundary, exactly one 'pad-dock' {padId} on the flip tick, dockTick × 50 ms < 15 s, settlement on the flat disc (≤ 20 m, |alt − padY| ≤ 1, |vel.y| < 2, regime 'surface'), wire state 'docked' {padId} + flightRegime 'surface', the one-pad invariant per tick, and 10 extra VTOL-held rest ticks with NO undock; (2) determinism — two fresh-shard runs give the EXACT same dock tick, final position and velocity (fixed seed, no wall clock, no Math.random).
- **TUNING (step 2) — the physics that made it converge:** the atmosphere regime has no main thruster and VTOL lift exactly cancels gravity (the pinned TASK-29.1 finding), so the approach is a two-phase glide: (a) GLIDE (up = 0) — 90 u/s inbound momentum + quadratic drag + gravity; the drag decay is logarithmic (x(t) = k⁻¹·ln(1 + k·v₀·t)), so a "few u/s" inbound stalls after ~10 m — 90 u/s dead-sticks the full 100 m from 50 m altitude (scanned a 26-combo grid; everything ≤ ~70 u/s froze 20–28 m short of the pad); (b) VTOL final phase (up = 1) — the key switches on ONLY when within 25 m AND below 2 m altitude: the committed ×0.5/tick assist kills the residual drift while the ship settles onto the flat 20 m disc; switching earlier would hover the ship (lift = gravity, no net descent) and switching at 100 m would freeze it short. Scripted as a pure state predicate `vtolKeyHeld(pos)` (distance + altitude above the padSurfaceHeight ground), documented as a block-comment phase script like shard.regime.test.ts. Measured: lands ~13 m from the pad center, rests at |v| ≈ 0.3 u/s.
- No UI change → no Playwright/screenshots (functional, tests-only task — the unit test IS the convergence test). No new dirs → STRUCTURE.md unchanged (tests are excluded).
Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` clean; eslint --fix + prettier --write on the touched file clean; targeted `npx vitest run src/server/shard/shard.pads.approach.test.ts` → 2 passed; full `npm run test` → 87 files, 787 passed / 1 skipped / 0 failed (~86 s).

### 2026-10-01 — TASK-29.1: Pad + docking tests — derivation, hysteresis, dock/takeoff transitions, one-pad invariant
Tests-only task for the committed TASK-29 server pad machine; one REAL bug found and fixed minimally (with regression coverage).
- `app/src/shared/world/pads.test.ts` (NEW, 20 tests) — pure pad math: `padsForSystem` (exactly one pad per landable planet in orbital-slot order; deterministic per (seed, system) and differing across seeds; world pos = planetAnchor(index) + chunk (0,0) local pad offset, INSIDE the 1 km atmosphere; height = the terrain cell at the pad's world position; second call returns the same cached array). `resolvePadTarget` (nearest within 20 m incl. boundary; hysteresis — UNTRACKED 19 m acquires / 21 m no, TRACKED kept to 25 m incl. and released just past, kept even when another pad is closer until release; ties break on padId independent of list order; at most one pad ever; unknown current id falls back to nearest-in-range). `satisfiesDock` clause-by-clause (range ≤ 20 incl.; regime EXACTLY 'surface'; |vel.y| < 2 strict both directions; |alt − padY| ≤ 1 incl.). VTOL assist gate by gate (up > 0; speed < 50 strict; horizontal ≤ 100 m incl., altitude ignored, no pads → never) and `applyVtolAssist` (x/z ×0.5, y untouched, input never mutated). `padSurfaceHeight` (flat at pad height ≤ 20 m incl. boundary; exact raised-cosine midpoint at 25 m; real terrain at ≥ 30 m; strictly monotonic blend in between; no pad → terrain).
- `app/src/server/shard/shard.pads.test.ts` (NEW, 4 sim scenarios through the REAL SimLoop, shard.regime.test.ts conventions: stub repo/bus/log, addEntity + registerConnection, one tick per `sim.step(25 + n*50)`, `padsForSystem` as the only source of the pad position, never hardcoded): (a) DOCK — VTOL-off drop from 60 m above the pad center onto the flat disc → 'pad-dock' {padId} on exactly the tick the state flipped + `entityToState` regime 'docked' + padId + flightRegime 'surface'; (b) TAKEOFF — from docked, scripted vel.y = 5 u/s (v1 atmosphere cannot climb on VTOL alone — VTOL_LIFT = GRAVITY exactly) → 'pad-undock' within ONE tick, wire state back to 'sublight'; (c) HYSTERESIS — docked ship teleported (dev hook) to 23 m on the blended pad-terrain keeps its pad with NO undock event; 26 m releases within one tick; (d) INVARIANT — across dock → band → release → re-dock, `entity.padId` is ever at most one id and 'pad-dock' never re-fires while docked (checked per tick; exactly 2 docks / 1 undock for the scripted run).
- **REAL BUG (fixed minimally, regression = scenarios c+d):** the committed `updatePadState` re-ran the FULL `satisfiesDock` (incl. the ≤ 20 m range) for ALREADY-DOCKED ships, so a docked ship drifting into the 20–25 m band was undocked every tick — the exact flap the hysteresis band exists to prevent. Fix in `shard.ts`: a docked ship KEEPS its tracked pad while `resolvePadTarget` keeps it (≤ 25 m) AND regime is still 'surface' AND |vel.y| < 2; a fresh dock still requires the full condition. The `resolvePadTarget` hysteresis (already correct in pads.ts) now actually governs the sim.
- **PHYSICS FINDING (pinned in the test file header):** the v1 atmosphere model has NO main thruster and VTOL lift exactly cancels gravity (`VTOL_LIFT = GRAVITY`, up ≤ 1) — VTOL can decelerate a fall to a hover but can NEVER bring a ship down. Any "hold VTOL to settle" approach stalls the ship at altitude (the first draft of the re-approach phase hovered at 40 m for the full 4000-tick cap). Docking approaches must be VTOL-OFF drops; the ground clamp zeroes vel.y → 'surface' (shard.regime.test.ts 'coast' precedent). VTOL assist math is covered at the unit level instead.
- No UI change → no Playwright/screenshots (functional, tests-only task). No new dirs → STRUCTURE.md unchanged (tests are excluded).
Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` clean; `npx vitest run src/shared/world/pads.test.ts src/server/shard/shard.pads.test.ts` → 29 passed; eslint --fix + prettier --write on the 3 touched files clean; full `npm run test` → 86 files, 785 passed / 1 skipped / 0 failed (~86 s).

The last literal TASK-28 AC is now met in the RENDER test (the pure-math counterpart in planets.test.ts already was): the real atmosphere dome, driven by the shared haze numbers, is rendered through a REAL WebGL renderer (headless Playwright) for a minimum-haze and a maximum-haze atmospheric planet of the seeded galaxy, and the density scaling is asserted to be visible in the rendered pixels AND to match the shared-math mix exactly.

Two probe-local overrides make the expected pixel EXACT (dome module untouched): raw-sRGB clear via `setClearColor(new THREE.Color(r/255,…))` (the numeric ctor does no ColorManagement conversion, unlike `scene.background`/hex ctors) + raw uAtmoColor/uSkyColor uniform override, and `THREE.NoBlending` (the shader's alpha = uHaze would otherwise double-apply the haze: clear·(1-h²) + atmo·h² ≠ lerp).

### 2026-10-01 — TASK-28.1: Atmosphere view — dome + skybox crossfade wiring (one-number, no-desync blend)
The live atmosphere transition is wired end to end: one shared haze number drives the dome IN and the skybox OUT, so they can never desync.
- `app/src/client/world/atmosphere-view.ts` (NEW) — pure `atmosphereViewFor(pos, system, current='space')`: null system / space regime → `{ planet: null, altitude: pos.y, boundary: 0, haze: 0 }`; else resolves the owning planet via `regimeFor` (live `current` keeps the [1000,1050) exit hysteresis band consistent with the sim) and computes `boundary = boundaryFactor(alt, planet)`, `haze = hazeFactor(alt, {radius, density})` from the SAME shared functions as the sim.
- `app/src/client/world/atmosphere-view.test.ts` (NEW, 4 tests) — mid-band boundary ≈ 0.5 with haze = 0.5·densityScale from the same shared fns; far/null-system → 0/0/null; exit band [1000,1050) respects `current`; airless planet → null/0/0.
- `app/src/client/world/WorldManager.ts` — owns the shared dome (`createAtmosphereDome(ATMOSPHERE_BOUNDARY_M)`, repositioned to the owning planet's anchor; hidden at zero cost in space), stores the system object, and `setAtmosphereView(pos, current)` drives dome haze + `sky.opacity = 1 - haze` + `stars.opacity = 0.95·(1-haze)` from ONE number. Tint via raw sRGB floats (`setFromHex01`, `THREE.NoColorSpace` — the shader writes gl_FragColor with no output transform).
- `app/src/client/main.tsx` — `useGameSession` gained optional `onAtmosphereView?: (pos, regime) => void`; the self entity_update handler calls it right after `onSelfUpdate` with the LIVE tracker regime. App bridges via a CLOSURE `(pos, regime) => worldRef.current?.setAtmosphereView(pos, regime)` (TDZ-safe: worldRef read only on WS messages, post-mount).
- **Star draw-order regression (found by e2e, fixed with explicit renderOrder):** making the skybox `transparent = true` (required for the opacity crossfade) moved it from the opaque pass into the transparent pass alongside the star Points. Three's `reversePainterSortStable` compares projected bounding-sphere-center z, which for two origin-centered shells (sky r=420 exact center; star shell's farthest-point centroid slightly offset) is a fragile near-tie that drew the full-opacity sky OVER the star points — flat starless skybox. Fix: explicit layering `sky.renderOrder = 0 < stars.renderOrder = 1 < dome.renderOrder = 2` (starfield.ts, atmosphere-dome.ts), which short-circuits the z tie-break and is physically correct (sky farthest → stars → dome closest).
- `app/tests/e2e/atmosphere-view.spec.ts` (NEW) — live-wiring smoke: claim + join, canvas must render non-uniformly (pixel-variance > 1) while 10 Hz self snapshots drive the new path, clean console, screenshot.
Verified: `npx vitest run` → 82 files, 744 passed / 1 skipped; `npm run typecheck` clean; `npm run lint` clean; e2e spec 1 passed (6.8 s). Screenshot: `.ralph/screenshots/TASK-28.1-1.png` (full starfield restored).

### 2026-10-01 — TASK-27: Continuous camera handoff across all regimes (CameraRig)
One `THREE.PerspectiveCamera` (FOV 75, constant) now serves every regime — no second camera, no swap risk. Cockpit → on-foot (and back) is a 600 ms eased animation along a precomputed 5-sample path that never enters terrain.
- `app/src/client/camera/pose-math.ts` (NEW) — pure, DOM-free handoff math on the shared `@shared/physics/vec` ops: `cockpitPose` (ship-local offset (0, 0.5, 1.2) rotated by ship quat, look = ship forward), `onFootPose` (head point = feet + 1.6 m as look target; camera 4 m behind along yaw/pitch), `lerpPose`, `slerpVec` (short-arc look slerp), `easeInOutCubic`, `clampPitchRad/Deg` (±80°), `nudgeOutOfTerrain` + `computeHandoffPath` (5 samples, position lerp + look slerp, each sample lifted to `heightAt + 1.5 m` when the straight path dips into geometry — x/z stay on the lerp line), `samplePath`. All deterministic (no Math.random).
- `app/src/client/camera/CameraRig.ts` (NEW) — owns the single camera. Steady state exponentially chases the active mode's target pose (position lerp + quaternion slerp, k = 8/s → the ~100 ms follow feel). `handoff(to)` precomputes the safe path from the CURRENT smoothed pose, locks user input for exactly 600 ms (look deltas dropped, gameplay input still accepted upstream), and lands on the final (nudged) path sample. Cancellable ONLY by a second handoff (which restarts from the first one's current pose — continuous); input can never cancel it. Injectable clock/heightAt/lifecycle hooks; FOV never touched.
- `app/src/client/camera/camera-debug.ts` (NEW) — dev-only `window.__CAMERA__` hook (same `import.meta.env.DEV` gate as `__DRIFT__`/`__STREAM__`). `handoffProbe()` scripts the rig standalone (fake ship 10 u up, character 20 u ahead, analytic mesa the straight path must clear, fixed 10 ms clock), reports the full contract (start/end counts, lock window, 5-sample nudged path, pitch clamp, FOV), and renders the final on-foot view into `#__camera-probe-canvas` (preserved buffer) for the screenshot. No real disembark flow needed until TASK-31/35.
- `app/src/client/main.tsx` — installs `installCameraDebug()` next to the other hooks (2 lines; no-op in prod).
- Tests: `pose-math.test.ts` (18 — pose construction, lerp endpoints/clamp, slerp short-arc/antipodal, ease anchors/symmetry, pitch clamp ±80°, nudge lift/no-touch-xz, 5-sample path with exact endpoints + exactly-the-dipping-samples-lifted against the mesa, flat-ground pure lerp, samplePath) and `camera-rig.test.ts` (12 — **exactly one animation** per mode switch (one start + one end, no more on later frames), **input lock** (deltas rejected mid-anim, accepted after, ignored in cockpit, zero yaw drift during lock), exactly 600 ms (locked at 590, unlocked at 600), camera sits on path sample 3 of 5 at t = 300 ms, lands on the on-foot pose, reverse handoff, second-handoff-cancels (input can't), pitch clamp, k = 8/s follow lag + convergence + yaw orbit, no-op handoff to same mode). `tests/e2e/camera-handoff.spec.ts` (NEW) — Playwright smoke via the hook: asserts the whole contract in-page and screenshots the rendered on-foot view. Live dev-server smoke (separate script) also green with zero console errors.
- Verified: `npx vitest run src/client/camera/` → 30 passed; full `npm run test` → 79 files, 721 passed / 1 skipped / 0 failed; `npm run typecheck` clean; `npm run lint` (eslint + prettier) clean; `npx playwright test --config playwright.e2e.config.ts camera-handoff` → 1 passed (13.9 s) — reported `starts=1 ends=1 lockWindow=600ms nudges=2 terrainClear=true pitchClamped=80.0° fov=75 rendered=true`. ### 2026-10-01 — TASK-26.1: Streaming pipeline — three world test files green
All TASK-26 working tree landed and committed. The drafted tests exposed FOUR real pipeline bugs (not just the two known from the failed attempts):
1. `chunks.ts` queue loop: `else { break }` spent only ONE work unit per frame even when the 4 ms budget was unspent (slices measured 0.2–0.9 ms) — the queue crawled at 1 unit/frame, so the forward column starved the near-diagonals ahead → 59 pop-in frames. Fix: keep working the in-flight build until the budget is actually spent (~4 units/frame; a 15-unit chunk takes ~4 frames, not ~15).
2. `chunks.ts` `activeSet` at rest used radius 1 → 9 chunks instead of the contracted 13 (3x3 + 4 cardinals two away); the c===2 filter could never fire. Fixed radius to 2.
3. `chunks.ts` `parseChunkKey` accepted malformed keys (`Number('') === 0` — '', ',5', '1,' did not throw). Now a strict `/^(-?\d+),(-?\d+)$/` gate.
4. THE BIG ONE — impostor→full upgrade: chunks cached as far-ring impostors (horizon band, 2–8 km) were NEVER rebuilt when the player approached and they entered the active set (`cached.has` skipped scheduling). `isReady` said true, but `geometries.near/mid` were null → steady-state flight rendered 0 near/mid tris (all flat quads). Fix: the active-schedule loop now schedules a full ChunkBuild alongside the cached impostor; on completion the cache entry is replaced and the old quad geometry disposed (scene swaps its mesh pointer on the next sync — no blank frame, swap happens ~960 m out, far beyond the 100 m edge contract).
Benchmark (step 1): defined `MAX_SLICE_BUDGET_MS = 8` (AC2 per-slice contract) and finished the percentile work — per-frame slice ms collected, assert p99 < 8 (a single GC spike in the max — measured 7.6–8.3 ms vs p99 5.4 ms — must not fail the run); logs record max + p99 + avg, per-frame triangle max, LRU peak (400/400, 72.1 MB est). Steady state now reads near 73,728 + mid 81,920 + far 452 (all 49 active full) — two consecutive runs both pass (2.6–2.7 s wall).
Hang caps (step 2): every unbounded wait loop now fails fast with the stuck chunk named — `buildToDone` (100 units + stage), `warmToNearBlock` + LOD-swap wait (4000 frames + pending), benchmark pre-warm (4000 frames + missing keys). No hang observed; the combined 3-file run completes in ~4.6 s (the old 300 s hang came from the one-unit-per-frame crawl + uncapped waits).
Test-side fixes: `chunkCenterOffset(-1,2,500,500)` expectation was a draft typo (center (-160,800) − player (500,500) = (-660,300)); the resting-cardinal key list wasn't in true JS string-sort order; the gauge test asserted `warned[0]` (the backlog drop warning now fires during warm-up with the 13-chunk set) instead of the surface-tris warning specifically; removed two dead benchmark helpers (`EDGE_MARGIN_M`, `distToChunkBox` — superseded by the stronger 3x3 check).
Verified: 3 world files green alone AND combined; `npm run test` → 77 files, 691 passed / 1 skipped; `npm run typecheck` clean; `npx eslint .` clean; prettier applied. STRUCTURE.md gained the 3 pipeline modules.

### 2026-10-01 — TASK-25.2: Regime manager client wiring + TASK-25 closeout
TASK-25 is now COMPLETE. The running client tracks its own regime: one `RegimeTracker` + one `ControlsRemapper` instantiated from the game session (tracker local prediction + server authority; remap + debug log on every regime change).
- `app/src/client/state/regime-wiring.ts` (NEW) — `RegimeWiring`: owns the tracker + remapper. `setSystem(seed, systemId)` → tracker.reset() + setPlanets(systemRegimePlanets(systemForId(seed, systemId))); `onSelfUpdate(entity, nowMs)` → optional `flightRegime` = server authority (missing = no authority, v1 back-compat), then local prediction from the last known flight state (pos, |vel|) — the stand-in until TASK-26's per-frame local sim; onRegimeChange → remapper.setRegime (instant swap + debug log). Injectable warn/log sinks (DOM-free, clock-free).

All pre-existing regime/controls tests unchanged and green.

### 2026-10-01 — TASK-25.1: Regime manager — fix the 2 stale atmosphere fixtures, full suite green
The last unmet TASK-25 acceptance criterion ('npm run test green') is now met. The two failing fixtures in `app/src/server/shard/shard.test.ts` spawned ships ~10 km from the planet anchor (10000, 0), so the new per-tick `resolveRegime` correctly flipped them to 'space' (no gravity) on tick 1. Fixed the fixtures, not the regime machine:
- 'integrates atmosphere ships against chunk-cached terrain (O(1) heightAt)' — spawn + height probe moved from (40, 40) to (10040, 40): the ship now spawns ~57 u from the anchor, inside the 1000 u atmosphere boundary, and the straight-down fall keeps shrinking d. Both original assertions (fell; pos.y >= ground - 1e-6, no tunneling) unchanged.

The overlay only mounts under `import.meta.env.DEV` — prod builds never ship it.

App-wide `frameMonitor` singleton.

Mounted in `main.tsx` under `import.meta.env.DEV`.

No loading screen: the canvas renders the whole time.

Rejections leave the player exactly where they were.

- `app/src/client/state/warp.ts` — TASK-7 bus kept + phase store (`idle|warping-in|awaiting|warp-out`) + `WarpController` (2 s in → race request vs 10 s timeout → 2 s out; `start()` busy-guard, `abort()`, injectable delay, 'System full' toast mapping).

Carries the SERVER-provided seed from `/api/health` (`ready`/`seed`) and derives `starChart(count)` / `planetList(starId)` in-page via the real shared generators — so the e2e exercises the client's actual derivation path. `main.tsx` calls `installDriftDebug()` at module load and `reportServerSeed(h.galaxySeed)` when health answers.

Timing-independent by construction (compared data is a pure function of the seed — no wall-clock fields), so it cannot flake on load order/latency.

### 2026-09-30 — TASK-70: Playwright e2e harness (two-client multiplayer, self-booting server)
The e2e harness is now self-contained: `npm run test:e2e` boots the REAL dev server itself (vite app + API/WS on random free ports, sqlite DB in a fresh tmp dir per test file), drives the claim flow in a real headless Chromium, and verifies the core multiplayer behaviors — no pre-started `npm run dev` required.
- `app/scripts/dev-test.mjs` (NEW) + `dev:test` npm script — boots vite + the tsx server side by side like `npm run dev`, but ports/DB come from env (`VITE_PORT`/`API_PORT`/`DB_PATH`); forwards SIGTERM/SIGINT to both children with an 8 s SIGKILL fallback. `vite.config.ts` gains env-overridable port + proxy target (defaults unchanged).
- `app/tests/e2e/fixtures.ts` (NEW) — worker-scoped Playwright fixture: two free ports + `mkdtemp` sqlite, spawns `npm run dev:test` detached (process-group kill on teardown), polls `/api/health` through the vite proxy (40 s boot budget), yields `{baseURL, apiPort}`. `playwright.e2e.config.ts` (NEW): testDir `tests/e2e`, globalTimeout 60 s, 1 worker (whole suite shares one server), 20 s test timeout, `retain-on-failure` traces. `npm run test:e2e` → new config; the legacy `:3000`-targeting specs are excluded from it (`testIgnore tests/e2e/**` in the old config) and vice versa — the unit `npm run test` (vitest) never touched either.
- `app/tests/e2e/pages/claim.ts` + `pages/game.ts` + `base-page.ts` + `helpers.ts` (NEW, per e2e-tester skill) — page objects for the claim screen (`getByLabel`/`getByRole`) and in-system UI (canvas, player list, Enter-toggled chat); helpers: unique timestamp callsigns, console-error collectors, and the GL `readPixels` luminance-variance sampler (best-of-four 32x32 regions — reads the drawing buffer, so DOM HUD never interferes).
- `app/src/client/render/starfield.ts` (NEW) — the canvas finally has something to render: a deterministic three.js starfield (shared PRNG, `seedFromString`/`hash2`) — gradient sky-sphere + 2500 pixel-constant point sprites on a 150–200 u shell, slow drift, `preserveDrawingBuffer` so e2e can sample outside the rAF loop. Mounted in `main.tsx` on `#game-canvas`; placeholder until the TASK-26 streaming pipeline takes over. Unit test (5 cases): bit-identical same-seed generation, seed sensitivity, shell radius bounds, color spread.
- Tests (3 total, ~42 s incl. one server boot, under the 90 s budget): core-flow.spec.ts — claim → session → join → canvas variance > 1 (headless WebGL renders, non-uniform) → player list shows "(you)"; multiplayer.spec.ts — two contexts see each other in the presence list (mirror view + screenshot), and a chat message reaches the peer in < 2 s (delivery measured after A's local send).
- Gotchas hit: **default headless Chromium (SwiftShader) needed NO extra GL flags** — documented in the config comments (the `--use-gl=angle --use-angle=swiftshader` escape hatch is recorded there if a future environment goes black). First chat run measured 2062 ms because the timer started BEFORE the local typing — moved `t0` after the send. Playwright fixtures: worker-scoped extend requires the key in the 2nd generic slot (`extend<object, E2eFixtures>`) and the runtime mandates an object-destructuring first arg (`{}, use`). The 2 s budget includes expect's polling, so delivery is timed with a 50 ms `waitForFunction` + elapsed assert.
- Verified: `tsc --noEmit` clean, `eslint` + `prettier --check` clean, `npm run test` → 57 files / 537 tests all pass (was 532; +5 starfield), `npm run test:e2e` → 3 passed (42 s), legacy `playwright --list` still shows exactly the 4 :3000 specs. No stray processes or tmp dirs after teardown.
- Screenshots: `.ralph/screenshots/TASK-70-1.png` (core flow: starfield + "(you)" row + 1 aboard), `TASK-70-2.png` (presence: both callsigns, "(you)" markers, join toast), `TASK-70-3.png` (chat: peer view of the log).

### 2026-09-30 — TASK-17: Reconnect and full state resync
A dropped connection no longer resets the world: the ship keeps living in the shard (idle, coasting under the same 20 Hz physics) while the player is away; on reconnect the same token re-joins the same system and receives a full resync snapshot with the ship at its current (possibly moved-while-idle) position, no duplicate entities, and no full UI reset (chat log + player list preserved). The TASK-12 grace/reap rules are unchanged — a long absence re-joins the reloaded shard at the last flushed position.
- `app/src/server/shard/shard.ts` — step 1 "idle continuation": the tick now iterates `playerEntities` directly, so an input-less ship keeps being integrated (coasts, applies drag/collision) instead of freezing; entities carry an `idle` flag set when their connection leaves. Stale-conn guards: `registerConnection` supersedes any zombie connection for the same playerId, and `unregister`/`leave`/`enqueueInput` are all source-checked (an input or close from a stale connId is dropped with a debug log — a late frame from a dead socket can't idle or move the ship); `adoptEntity` un-idles on (re)adoption.
- `app/src/server/ws.ts` + `app/src/server/index.ts` — `onGameMessage` now carries `source: conn` so input routing can pass the originating connection to `shard.enqueueInput` (the stale-conn drop path).
- `app/src/client/net/session.ts` (REWRITTEN, step 2 "reconnect resync") — lazy dial, `ConnectionState` (`connecting/connected/reconnecting/lost`), auto-retry with exponential backoff (1 s base, ×2, capped 5 s — retry is injectable for tests), a 30 s "lost" window after which the state flips to `lost` and the UI may show the overlay, `retryNow()` for the Retry button, and `onSnapshot(snapshot, reconnect)` so the host rebuilds PredictionEngine + RemoteBuffers + presence on a resync while keeping same-system UI state. A deliberate `close()` never retries; a refused rejoin (error frame during the auto `joinSystem`) closes the socket and rolls back into the next backoff dial.
- `app/src/client/net/chat.ts` — `mergeSnapshot()`: on resync, append only messages newer than the current tail (ts watermark) and emit only when something was added — the chat log is never cleared while staying in the same system.
- `app/src/client/net/presence.ts` — `reconnected()`: fires a one-shot 'reconnected' toast (zero-change → no emit, list untouched).
- `app/src/client/main.tsx` — `ConnectionLostOverlay`: full-screen `#connection-lost-overlay` with a `#reconnect-retry` button, rendered only in state `lost`; status line gains "· reconnecting…"/"· connection lost" suffixes; resync keeps the chat log + player list when the system is unchanged. The overlay does not block the ESC menu (TASK-17 note).
- Tests (step 3): `app/src/server/galaxy/reconnect.ws.test.ts` (NEW, live ws, 3 tests) — (a) mid-flight drop: thrust 5 ticks, close → peer gets presence leave, ship goes `idle === true` after 2 s and moved > 1 u while idling, reconnect snapshot has exactly the same entity count (no duplicate), continuity `dist(snapPos, pStop + vStop·Δt) < 1 u`, peer gets presence join; (b) zombie conn: a same-token re-join supersedes the old connection (one conn, one entity), the zombie's thrust is DROPPED (velocity stays ~0) and its late close leaves the live conn + non-idle entity intact; (c) reap-and-rejoin with a fake clock: fly ~1 s, close, advance past the 60 s grace, `reapEmpty()` → system gone, DB row `state === 'flying'`, re-join → generation 2, snapshot position within `|v|·1.5 + 1` of the flushed row, > 10 u from the dock. `app/src/client/net/session.test.ts` (NEW, 7 tests, scripted fake WebSocket + fake timers) — retry at base + resync with `onSnapshot(_, true)`, backoff 10/20/40/cap, 'lost' only on a retry step after the 30 s window, refused-rejoin recovery, deliberate close never retries, initial-connect failure is a caller error (no retry), `send()` no-op while down, hello/auth/join on every dial. `chat.test.ts` +2 (mergeSnapshot appends only newer-than-tail, ring cap, no-op/no-emit), `presence.test.ts` +1 (`reconnected()` toast). `app/tests/reconnect.spec.ts` (NEW, Playwright) — init-script WebSocket proxy: `__dropCurrent()` closes only the game socket (`url.includes('/ws')` — vite's HMR socket must survive), `__wsBlock` makes game dials return a stub that fires onerror/onclose(1006) after 20 ms so no real resync can form. Flow: claim+join → send chat → drop → status `<p>` shows "reconnecting" → 30 s → overlay + retry button (screenshot 1) → unblock → auto-resync ≤ 5 s → 'reconnected' toast, chat preserved, claim form + overlay gone (screenshot 2), zero console errors.
- Gotchas hit: `ctx.setOffline(true)` does NOT sever an established Chromium WebSocket (the status line never changed) — the proxy approach above; killing a blocked retry dial 50 ms after construction didn't work either (the local join handshake finishes in ~10 ms, so the session resynced→killed→resynced and `downSince` reset on every resync so 'lost' never fired — hence the failing STUB socket); the reconnect/lost text renders in the status `<p>` ("server ok — seed …"), not in `#sys-id`; the shard never empties while a peer is in-system (assert on `entity.idle`, not `connections.size === 0`); `POST /api/callsigns` does return `playerId` (widen the claim() cast).
- Verified: `tsc --noEmit` clean, `eslint --fix` + `prettier --write` clean, full `npm run test` → 56 files / 532 tests all pass, `npx playwright test` (full e2e suite) all pass.
- Screenshots: `.ralph/screenshots/TASK-17-1.png` (ConnectionLostOverlay with Retry button after 30 s), `.ralph/screenshots/TASK-17-2.png` (auto-resynced: toast 'reconnected', chat preserved, overlay gone).

### 2026-09-30 — TASK-16: System text chat with history and sanitization
System-scoped text chat: every connection in a shard sees every message as `{from, text, ts}` (server-assigned ms epoch, strictly monotonic), sanitized server-side, rate-limited per connection, and rendered as inert React text. No channels, no private messages, no persistence (v1).
- `app/src/shared/chat.ts` (NEW) — contract: `CHAT_MAX_CHARS=200` (after trim), `CHAT_WINDOW_MS=10_000` / `CHAT_WINDOW_MAX=5`, `CHAT_HISTORY_MAX=100`; `sanitizeChatText()` strips C0/DEL/C1 controls, ANSI CSI sequences (CSI alternative ordered FIRST so an ESC never matches alone and leaves `[31m` garbage), bidi overrides, zero-width/format chars, and trims.
- `app/src/shared/protocol/schemas.ts` — `chatInboundSchema` (`{text}`, strict, 1..200 after trim) + `chatMessageSchema` (`{from, text, ts}` ms epoch, strict); `messageSchemas.chat` is the UNION of both (required so the client's `parseMessage` accepts the server's broadcast shape); enter_system snapshot `chat` array carries the new shape. Old `CHAT_CHANNELS` + 280-char/ISO-ts shape removed.
- `app/src/server/ratelimit.ts` — `ChatLimiter` gains an options param `{minGapMs, maxChars, windowMs, windowMax}` (TASK-65 defaults preserved); `ws.ts` instantiates TASK-16 rules (`minGapMs: 0`, 200 chars, 5/10 s) as `conn.chatLimiter`.
- `app/src/server/ws.ts` — chat flow: sanitize → empty-after-sanitize = `invalid-message` (counts toward the invalid-message drop) → limiter check → `rate-limited` (existing 3-in-10 s escalation) → `onGameMessage(conn, 'chat', {text})`.
- `app/src/server/shard/shard.ts` — `handleChat(from, text)`: ts = `max(now, lastChatTs+1)` (strictly monotonic), 100-message ring buffer, encode-once broadcast to ALL shard connections including the sender; `chatHistory()`. `app/src/server/galaxy/router.ts` `enterSnapshot` now ships `shard.chatHistory()` (late joiners get the last 100).
- `app/src/server/index.ts` — `onGameMessage` routes `chat` to `shard.handleChat(conn.callsign, text)`.
- `app/src/client/net/chat.ts` (NEW) — `ChatStore`: 100-message ring buffer, `loadSnapshot()` clears on system change + drops stale frames via a ts watermark, emits only on change.
- `app/src/client/hud/chat-log.tsx` (NEW) — `ChatLog`: top-left column (below the title card, above the player list), `[HH:MM] CALLSIGN: text`, text-only rendering (no innerHTML); Enter toggles the hidden input (Enter sends + closes, Esc closes); auto-scroll pins to the bottom only while the user is already there. BUG FIXED here after the first e2e run: React 18 flushes the keydown state update synchronously, so by the time the same Enter event bubbled to the window-level "open input" listener the input was already unmounted (`inputRef.current === null`) and it REOPENED the input in the same keystroke — the window handler now matches the (possibly detached) target by `id="chat-input"`.
- `app/src/client/main.tsx` — feeds `ChatStore` from `chat` frames, `loadSnapshot(snapshot.chat)` after join, renders `<ChatLog>` in-system, sends `clientRef.current?.send('chat', {text})`.
- Tests: `src/shared/chat.test.ts` (NEW) — sanitizer cases incl. ANSI CSI ordering, bidi, zero-width, trim-to-empty. `src/client/net/chat.test.ts` (NEW) — ring buffer, snapshot watermark, change-only emits. `src/server/chat.integration.test.ts` (NEW, real child server) — 3 clients: 6 interleaved messages all delivered in identical order with strictly increasing ts; 6th-in-10 s dropped with `rate-limited` to the sender only; per-connection isolation; control-char stripping; XSS payload arrives as inert text; empty/oversize/non-string/blank → `invalid-message`; late joiner's `enter_system` snapshot carries the accumulated history. 3 `handleChat` unit tests in `shard.test.ts`; TASK-16 window case in `ratelimit.test.ts`; rewritten chat case in `ratelimit-flood.test.ts` (no `channel` field, 5/10 s, 200 chars); updated chat fixtures in `schemas.test.ts` + `protocol.test.ts`.
- `app/src/server/galaxy/router.ws.test.ts` — fixed a PRE-EXISTING flake in the 16-player cap test (unrelated to TASK-16): `homeSystemIdForPlayer` hashes the random UUID, so ~13% of runs a Full-* claimant's docked starter ship belongs to the "quiet" system and the quiet shard rehydrates it (snapshot had 2 entities, not 1). The assertion now allows exactly that: every quiet-snapshot entity is the late player's ship or a claimant's ship whose home system IS the quiet system (same "allowed" logic as the 10-client test; no other assertion weakened).
- `app/tests/chat.spec.ts` (NEW, Playwright) — two browser contexts in one system: Enter-toggled input, both logs show `[HH:MM] CALLSIGN: hello drift`, the `<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>` payload renders as inert text with `#chat-log img` count 0, zero console errors in both contexts.
- Verified: `tsc --noEmit` clean, `eslint --fix` + `prettier --write` clean, full `npm run test` → 54 files / 516 tests all pass, `npx playwright test tests/chat.spec.ts` → 1 passed.
- Screenshots: `.ralph/screenshots/TASK-16-1.png` (A: shared log, both messages incl. inert XSS text, player list below), `.ralph/screenshots/TASK-16-2.png` (B: mirror view).

- `app/src/client/hud/toast-stack.tsx` (NEW) — "CALLSIGN joined/left", CSS 3 s fade-in/hold/fade-out, 3 visible max with the rest queued and promoted on dismiss, aria-live.
- `app/src/client/main.tsx` — callsign claim form (POST /api/callsigns, localStorage session, 409 → inline error), session boot: WS → join (`?sys=` URL override for e2e/dev) → PresenceStore fed from snapshot + presence events; `#sys-id` status line shows system id + live occupancy (App subscribes to the store — presence events only).
